# 👤 Absolute Acolyte Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Rift Stalkers (Bristle Woods Rift)

> **Group / Faction**: Rift Stalkers (Bristle Woods Rift)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Bristle Woods Rift  
> **Vulnerable To**: Rift  
> **Favored Bait**: Runic String Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 1,000,000 Points | 300,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Phenomenal cosmic power bound only by the Bristle Woods Rift, these terrifying mice of immense influence and control over time are a mere whisker away from breaking free from their confinements. Entwined in endless strings of time, altered by previous attempts at escape, it is unknown if the Absolute Acolyte Mouse will be able to eventually break free or perhaps already has, but we must do everything in our power to prevent this from occurring in any timeline!"

# 👤 Acolyte Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: The Forgotten Mice

> **Group / Faction**: The Forgotten Mice  
> **Primary Habitat**: Acolyte Realm  
> **Vulnerable To**: Forgotten  
> **Favored Bait**: Runic Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 31,850 Points | 36,309 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Years ago, the Acolyte mice brought necromancy to the Kingdom. Their undead creations rose from the depths of the Catacombs, with the sole purpose of destroying Gnawnia. Hunters’ lore states that the first-ever MouseHunter devised a way to turn the spells of the Forgotten Book against the Acolytes and banish them to another realm of existence, somewhere between the dead and the living. Had it not been for Plankrun’s heroic efforts, the small village of Gnawnia may never have become the prosperous town it is today. Now that they have returned, the Acolyte mice are amassing their creations, preparing to achieve their dark destiny: the eradication of each and every MouseHunter!"

## 🧀 Kebiasaan Umpan & Perilaku Berburu
### Cheese

The Acolyte Mouse is attracted only to Runic Cheese.

# 👤 Ascended Elder Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Rift Stalkers (Furoma Rift)

> **Group / Faction**: Rift Stalkers (Furoma Rift)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Furoma Rift  
> **Vulnerable To**: Rift  
> **Favored Bait**: Ascended Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 400,000 Points | 80,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "With lives nearing completion, their bodies have grown weak in their old age but their spirits have grown strong, so strong that they can barely be contained in any physical realm. Focused on finding "oneness" through menial but meaningful work, their training has come full circle, back to where students begin. Sweeping the grand floors of the Grand Pagoda, they find greater meaning, purpose, humility and spiritual wholeness through this act of cleanliness and self-sacrifice.<br>They may look hapless and powerless, but don't let that fool you... Striking one down will make them become more powerful than you can possibly imagine."

# 👤 Balack the Banished

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Balack's Banished

> **Group / Faction**: Balack's Banished  
> **Primary Habitat**: Balack's Cove  
> **Vulnerable To**: Forgotten  
> **Favored Bait**: Vengeful Vanilla Stilton  
> **Reward Tier**: 80,000 Points | 41,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Once, the three tribes, Nerg, Derr and Elub, were one, united under a ruler so cruel and merciless that the tribes-mice lived in constant fear of his whims. The Elders formulated a plan to banish Balack’s spirit to a remote cove near the Jungle of Dread, but he had other plans. He allowed the Elders to banish him, but he corrupted their enchantment along the way, pulling his attackers into the cove with him to be his servants for the rest of eternity."

# 👤 Cartographer

> **Entity**: Character

> Cartographers sell and repair maps. Because their skills are rare, buying or repairing a map can be quite an expensive process.

Cartographers are masters of patching damaged maps. Their rare skill takes a long time to learn and is often passed down from generation to generation. Although the hopes of finding routes to new hunting locations is exciting, the tedious nature of restoring maps makes hiring a cartographer rather expensive.

Hunters will find they need special Maps and Keys to unlock new regions and adventures as they travel the kingdom. Occasionally, a Cartographer's skill is required to repair damaged maps or obtain rare keys. In order to enlist a Cartographer's help, a hunter must provide the item they wish to have restored (dropped as loot) as well a payment in gold.

With the start of Season 3 of treasure hunting, Cartographers began offering Scroll Cases for area-specific Treasure Maps in exchange for Ancient Relic.

## History and Trivia

- 25 March 2014: Vacant Lot Cartographer was opened for hunters to purchase Rift Detector with Drained Rocket Pack, Exploded Lightshow, Inert Riftwood and 25,000 gold to access Gnawnia Rift.
- 29 July 2015: Cartographers no longer have wait times. Each map piece used to require a certain length of time for the Cartographer to restore.
- 29 July 2015: King's Gauntlet Floorplan is sold at Bazaar instead of Harbour.

# 👤 Charm Shoppe

> **Entity**: Character

Enhance your bait with magical charms that can boost the stats of your trap!
Using these charms along with your bait can give your trap boosted stats or special abilities.
Charms are consumed each time a piece of bait is used.

A Charm Shoppe is a shop which sells charms. Several regions include a permanent Charm Shoppe, and Events also tend to have a temporary Charm Shoppe. While the wording of the charm shoppe implies that charms are consumed on an encounter, there are some exceptions to the rule; read the "Consumed Upon" stat for a particular charm to see when it will be consumed.

## History and Trivia

- The Mountain Charm Shoppe was introduced on 12 January 2011 when Moustachio the Charmer returned to Gnawnia.
- On 25 January 2011, Charmbits became available to buy at the Mountain Charm Shoppe as Moustachio shared his secret to crafting Charms.
- The Crystal Library Charm Shoppe was introduced on 23 August 2011, fourteen days after the location release.
- The Haunted Terrortories Charm Shoppe was introduced on 19 October 2011 during the Halloween at the Haunted Terrortories event.
- The Festive Comet Charm Shoppe was introduced on 7 December 2011 during the Great Winter Hunt 2011 event.


---

# 👤 Cheese Shoppe

> **Entity**: Character

> Welcome to the Cheese Shoppe! Using better cheese attracts mice to your trap more often, so be sure to buy the best cheese you can afford, without exceeding the gold you earn from hunting. Want to save your gold and achieve the best attraction rate around? Buy SUPERlbrie+, and experience perks in attraction, crafting, and exclusive mice!

A Cheese Shoppe is a type of shop that offers various types of cheese for Hunters. Cheese is necessary for attracting mice to a trap setup..

# 👤 Deep Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Icewing's Invasion (Misc)

> **Group / Faction**: Icewing's Invasion (Misc)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Iceberg  
> **Vulnerable To**: Hydro  
> **Favored Bait**: No Preference  
> **Reward Tier**: 142,550 Points | 24,525 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Finally solving the question as to how the Iceberg is propelled, the Deep Mouse lives well below the Iceberg, dragging it relentlessly towards the shores of Gnawnia.Only the most dedicated hunters will ever be able to encounter this monster, let alone defeat it!"

## 💎 Rampasan Berharga (Loot Drops)
- **Drop Items**: Ancient RelicDeep Thought (25,000 points)Plankrun's Iceberg NotesSatchel of Gold (10,000 gold)


---

# 👤 Dojo Sensei

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Followers of Furoma

> **Group / Faction**: Followers of Furoma  
> **Primary Habitat**: Pinnacle Chamber  
> **Vulnerable To**: Tactical  
> **Favored Bait**: Onyx Gorgonzola  
> **Reward Tier**: 28,665 Points | 34,500 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Rarely seen, these wise old mice instruct the Dojo Masters from the shadows, lest their presence be known to MouseHunters. Hunting these mice is not for the faint of heart -- their swift attacks can leave the courage of even the most steadfast hunter extremely crippled. Hunters who take on the challenge of catching a Dojo Sensei and succeed are held in the highest regard, for they have conquered the forces of the Furoma region."

## 🧀 Kebiasaan Umpan & Perilaku Berburu
### Cheese

The Dojo Sensei is attracted only to Onyx Gorgonzola.

## 💎 Rampasan Berharga (Loot Drops)
- **Drop Items**: Ancient RelicDivine OrbsMaster's SealOnyx Mallet BlueprintsOnyx StoneSatchel of Gold (5,000)Satchel of Gold (10,000)


---

# 👤 Dragon Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Draconic Brood

> **Group / Faction**: Draconic Brood  
> **Primary Habitat**: Dracano  
> **Vulnerable To**: Draconic  
> **Favored Bait**: Inferno Havarti  
> **Reward Tier**: 74,200 Points | 39,940 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "High above the summit of Dracano circles the terrible Dragon mouse, scourge of the Tribal Isles. To this date, no trap has withstood the Dragon's fiery breath, and all attempts to hunt it have reduced the most advanced traps to smoking debris. Legend has it that the Acolyte Mice held the key to controlling the Dragon, but now that they are trapped in another realm, the Dragon mouse has expanded its territory. The Tribal Isles need a brave MouseHunter to confront this menace, before it sets its sights on Gnawnia!"

## 💎 Rampasan Berharga (Loot Drops)
- **Drop Items**: Ancient RelicDragon's ChestDragon Ember


---

# 👤 Eclipse Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Gauntlet Gladiators (Tier 8: The Eclipse)

> **Group / Faction**: Gauntlet Gladiators (Tier 8: The Eclipse)  
> **Primary Habitat**: King's Gauntlet  
> **Vulnerable To**: Forgotten  
> **Favored Bait**: Gauntlet Cheese Tier 8  
> **Reward Tier**: 379,000 Points | 300,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "This mouse cares not for cheese. To say the Eclipse Mouse commands the forces of darkness would be to underestimate its power, for it commands the very fear of darkness, liquifying hunters’ souls with a constant barrage of nightmares. It also turns traps against their owners, sending knives, lasers and even giant squid sailing through the air after opponents. It has only been trapped once before, and after it reached the King’s gauntlet, the hunter disappeared. Good luck."

## 💎 Rampasan Berharga (Loot Drops)
- **Drop Items**: Ancient Relic


---

# 👤 Ful'Mina the Mountain Queen

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Moussu Picchu Inhabitants (Storm Dragon)

> **Group / Faction**: Moussu Picchu Inhabitants (Storm Dragon)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Moussu Picchu  
> **Vulnerable To**: Draconic  
> **Favored Bait**: Dragonvine Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 120,000 Points | 30,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Resting high above Moussu Picchu among the clouds, Ful'Mina is the true queen of the mountain and ruler over the skies and storms. As the Moussu Picchu Inhabitants below battle endlessly for dominance of the weather, the resulting stalemate allows the queen to slumber for generations.<br>Once, long ago, she would appear high above the land anywhere a lightning cloud hovered, visiting the many realms of mice across Gnawnia, her tail smacking the ground like lightning as she plucked treasures with it to add to her hoard. Now, countless decades pass in between sightings of the Mountain Queen, so much so that her very existence has gone from folklore, to legend, to mere myth...<br>But now, MouseHunters from the Kingdom of Gnawnia have found their way through the mountain pass, and their presence has brought a shift to the power struggle in these ruins, as they always do. Hunters have begun to harness the powers of both the Wind Wanderer and Rain Roamer tribes of mice in the area, ironically bringing unity between the two after endless centuries of feuding.<br>As the winds grow stronger, the rains fall heavier, and the storms begin to mix and clash, great bone-shaking growls can be heard from above the thickest of clouds, where the queen is said to reside. Is this thunder? Or is it the great Mountain Queen being rudely awakened from her slumber? Slowly but surely, disturbed by the intensifying battle in the lands below and the low rumble of impending thunderstorms in the clouds upon which she rests, she begins to rouse from her deep sleep...<br>Soon, and without warning, Ful'Mina, the Mountain Queen, will descend upon the lands with great fury to bring order to the chaos through the destructive force of her indomitable lightning storms."

# 👤 General Store

> **Entity**: Character

> General Stores sell items that are used for crafting. Each General Store is unique to its location, and sells different items.

General Stores are located in several locations throughout the kingdom. Players may purchase items for crafting there. At times, Ronza's Traveling Shoppe has also carried supplies in a General Store.

# 👤 Heart of the Meteor

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Meteorite Miners (Dawn Destroyer)

> **Group / Faction**: Meteorite Miners (Dawn Destroyer)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Fort Rox  
> **Vulnerable To**: ArcaneShadow  
> **Favored Bait**: Sunrise Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 500,000 Points | 1,000,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Nestled within the remnants of the Fort Rox meteor slumbers an incredible source of power. This tiny mouse courses with energy, presumed to be what radiates throughout the Fort Rox mice, causing all of the unique transformations.<br>Seemingly at peace and unaware of her surroundings, she is in a deep and powerful trance, emitting waves of bright, blinding light to supply her followers with their great strength and abilities.<br>Nothing can break her trance, save for the alluring source of sunlight from Sunrise Cheese..."

# 👤 Icewing

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Icewing's Invasion (Misc)

> **Group / Faction**: Icewing's Invasion (Misc)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Iceberg  
> **Vulnerable To**: Hydro  
> **Favored Bait**: No Preference  
> **Reward Tier**: 51,250 Points | 11,295 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Legends tell of an ancient, hateful mouse, a child of wrath and ice, spoken about only in hushed tones around campfires to scare children and the weak-hearted. But this mouse is no fairy tale, and has a long and dark history with the Kingdom of Gnawnia.<br>Long ago, a powerful band of evil mice descended yearly upon Gnawnia in fearsome raids, stealing cheese and terrifying everyone. Icewing herself lead many a raid, and was next in line for rulership when the jealous chieftans banded together and cast her out, fearful that with her incredible power and cruelty, she would have done the same to them.<br>Over the years since, she has raised an army from the mice who remained loyal to her, and turned her sights to the south. Determined to prove that she can accomplish more without her traitorous clan holding her back, she intends to invade Gnawnia and see every mouse there swear fealty to her as their Queen. But first, she must wipe out the pesky humans who seem to get in the way of everything..."

# 👤 King

> **Entity**: Character

> "King" redirects here. For other uses, see King (disambiguation).

The King of Gnawnia has called brave mouse hunters to his kingdom to fight the mice that are destroying his lands. It is he that pays rewards to hunters. He also has the power to increase the title of a hunter. The King's name is currently a mystery, although speculation exists that his name has been determined by MouseHunt developers. A contest held in the Summer of 2008 garnered hundreds of guesses from players. When the contest ended, it was revealed by the developers that the King's name had not yet been correctly guessed.

The King knows how bad the mouse problem is in Gnawnia from personal experience. On 10 September 2008, the King's Crown was stolen by a Master Burglar Mouse. The Master Burglar Mouse was later caught by a lucky hunter, resulting in the greatest single prize awarded in MouseHunt to date.

When MouseHunt is added to Facebook profile tab (a feature which has since been disabled), the background image features the King as well as the Master Burglar mouse.

The King is the author of the Sandtail Call to Arms.


---

# 👤 Mythweaver

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Storytellers (Folklore Masterminds)

> **Group / Faction**: Storytellers (Folklore Masterminds)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Table of Contents  
> **Vulnerable To**: Forgotten  
> **Favored Bait**: Final Draft Derby Cheese  
> **Reward Tier**: 1,000,000 Points | 70,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Many a cautionary tale has been written about the dangers of hubris but few are as poignant or consequential as the one being penned by the Mythweaver.<br>This once meek and meager mouse began its story as a simple Bookborn willed into existence by the maniacal Zurreal the Eternal. There was something unique about this particular manifestation however and it caused Zurreal to take special notice. This Bookborn exhibited more intelligence, intuition, and understanding than the rest of its kind and so Zurreal decided to share small fragments of his immense knowledge with this curious creature. As the Bookborn consumed more knowledge, its abilities began to grow and so too did its usefulness. Zurreal recognized an opportunity to mold and shape this exceptional creation into a powerful assistant capable of expediting his plans to conquer humans and mice alike.<br>Unbeknownst to Zurreal, this creature was consuming knowledge at an unimaginable rate and before long, its intelligence and understanding had begun to surpass even its master's. This nameless creation eventually sought to have an identity of its own and, having developed a particular fascination with the countless volumes of folklore contained in the Crystal Library, began calling itself the Mythweaver. The Mythweaver began to recognize that he was merely a servant to an insane and tyrannical leader, a mere cog in a broken machine. And with that profound realization, this ethereal creature transformed into a unique and extraordinarily powerful mouse! On that day, the Mythweaver vowed to turn against his creator and become a true leader of mice; dedicated not only to their survival but to their supremacy in the land of Gnawnia!<br>So the Mythweaver scoured every inch of the great library in search of a means by which he could conquer the kingdom on behalf of his kind and one day he found what he had been searching for. Hidden inside a forgotten book on a forgotten shelf, the Mythweaver discovered a single page that Zurreal had missed all these years. This enchanted page was written by Zugzwang himself and on it he learned of a secret place of unprecedented power: the Folklore Forest!<br>He learned that deep in the Folklore Forest there exists a magical Book of Lore which contains the entire history of Gnawnia scribbled throughout its pages. What is special about this book however, is that if the words on these pages are changed then so too will history itself be changed. And thus began the Mythweaver's quest to rewrite history by writing the mice as the glorious champions and the humans as the vanquished foes!"

# 👤 Plankrun

> **Entity**: Character

The first ever Mouse Hunter; Plankrun was commissioned by the King to investigate the strange behaviors of the Mice appearing in the region of Gnawnia. His fate is, as of yet, unknown. However, pages torn from his journal have been found across the region, detailing his brave escapades. The page read by the Scribe Mouse mentions that he was known as Sir Plankrun.

# 👤 Retired Minotaur Mouse

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: Citizens of Zokor (Hidden Remnant)

> **Group / Faction**: Citizens of Zokor (Hidden Remnant)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Zokor  
> **Vulnerable To**: Forgotten  
> **Favored Bait**: None  
> **Reward Tier**: 1,000,000 Points | 1,000,000 Gold

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "Once young and lively, this minotaur loved racing through the hallways of the Labyrinth, chasing down hunters and mice alike, howling and bellowing with delight as they scurried at the mere sound of his hooves on the cobbled floors. But now, it seems he has run his last lap of the maze, and has settled down into cozy end-life routines. Donning his fuzzy slippers in his favourite easy chair (which is a giant sack of gold), this retired beast has had his pension paid out in full and has relocated to his secret inner chamber to enjoy his retirement in peace and quiet.<br>The same shenanigans that used to bring him joy now only cause him grief, and he can be seen shaking his fist from atop his tower at the other mice hustling and bustling in the city. All that noise, all that racket! What a grumpy old grump this once playful beast has become...<br>But even in his twilight and seemingly endless misery, he still manages to let a small grin spread across his lips whenever he causes a mouse to jump in fear at the sound of his snort or the clipping of his hooves.<br>No one has broken into his private chambers and lived to tell the tale. Rumor has it that he stores many great and valuable items on opulent displays. Perhaps a resourceful hunter can find a way in and nab one of these rare treasures before being caught!"

# 👤 Ronza

> **Entity**: Character

Ronza is a traveling merchant. The king allows her to sell exotic goods from her traveling shoppe, an airship. When she enters a region her location is an area available to hunters. Not much is known about her, though rumors are that she comes back on special occasions such as giveaways.

Nobody knows when (or if) she's coming back, what she will be selling, or how long she will stay.

## History and Trivia

In 2007 MouseHunt developer Jacob Johnson created a piece of artwork entitled The Captain, which was later adopted as the official Ronza image.

One of the books in Zurreal's Folly is titled Ronza.

It has been suggested that the carved initials on the Hapless Marionette refer to Ronza.

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "This elusive mouse makes his home in the damp subterranean rivers of the underpass, hiding in rocky crags. Its bat-like squeak and the splash of its feet can often be heard in caverns, but when hunters approach, it dives into the water and swims away faster than a Burroughs trout. Because it has spent so much time in seclusion, these guardians of the underpass have unusual taste--anyone aiming to catch it should note that only the gnarliest cheese will tempt it out of hiding."

# 👤 Trapsmith

> **Entity**: Character

> Trapsmiths sell weapons and bases that you can use to upgrade your trap's power, power bonus, luck, and attraction bonus. Power, power bonus and luck help you catch mice more often, and attraction bonus boosts your ability to attract mice. For safety reasons, Trapsmiths require you to meet strict points requirements in order to purchase higher-level traps. Visit other trapsmiths around the Kingdom to find new and more powerful weapons and bases.

A Trapsmith is a shop found in many locations. They are the places in MouseHunt for hunters to purchase new non-crafted weapons, bases and skins.

### Ronza's Traveling Shoppe Trapsmith

At Ronza's Traveling Shoppe, Ronza, the traveling merchant, appears on certain occasions, selling rare stock from her unknown homeland. On most of her visits, her trapsmith has offered rare, limited edition trap components. Note that the stock varies with each visit, and only items currently being sold can be refunded. For more information on what was sold during each visit, see the History and Trivia section in the location.

## History and Trivia

On 29 September 2010, the Meadow quit selling the Mouse Rocketine and NVMRC Forcefield Trap.

With the introduction of MouseHunt 3.0, several new trapsmiths were added, and existing shops changed prices and lineups for some consistency. In particular, the trapsmith formerly at the Lagoon relocated to the Great Gnarled Tree.

Before MouseHunt 3.0, Larry's description of a Trapsmith was:
Trapsmiths sell the tools of the MouseHunter trade. They are experts in creating powerful weapons and the bases required to mount them upon. Trapsmiths are most often retired MouseHunters who follow a line of tradition and therefore insist that a hunter has an appropriate level of experience before they will sell them a trap component.

Trapsmiths set up shops all around the world - as hunters travel to new regions and locations they will be able to buy from the unique stock of each shop.

> **Entity**: Character | **Faction**: The Marching Flame (Command)

> **Group / Faction**: The Marching Flame (Command)  
> **Primary Habitat**: Fiery Warpath  
> **Vulnerable To**: Physical

## 📜 Lore & Persona

> "The Warmonger is a most fearsome general of the Marching Flame. Harnessing an inferno blade, the Warmonger makes a sweeping conquest of all that stands in its path and gives unquestioned orders down its ranks. None yet have challenged this Warmonger and lived to tell the tale."
